CCT: Don't wait for a cookie restore when the OTR profile survived

An off-the-record Custom Tab (incognito or ephemeral) that is restored
from saved state defers creating its tab until
CookiesFetcher.restoreCookies() finishes on an AsyncTask, so that the
first page load sees the cookies. That was added for a task restored
after process death, where the cookies exist only in the encrypted file.

The same path runs when the activity is recreated in the foreground,
e.g. after a move to another display. There the off-the-record profile
is kept alive and still holds the cookies, so the restore is redundant.
The delay means the tab model has no current tab when
ExclusiveAccessManager.initialize() runs, and saved fullscreen is not
re-entered.

Skip the restore when a tab that is waiting to be reparented already
uses this Custom Tab's off-the-record profile. Such a tab cannot exist
after process death, so that path is unchanged. Profile existence alone
is not used as the signal, because startup code may create the profile
before this point.

The cookie file is then not deleted at restore; it is overwritten on
the next pause, as before.

Expose downloadable models and InstallCapability in OnDeviceModelControl

- Rename GetInstalledModels -> GetModels and InstalledModel -> ModelInfo
- Add OnDeviceModelControl::ModelStatus (kReady, kDownloadable); we will
  follow up with downloading/updating model status.
- Add OnDeviceModelControl::InstallCapability(OnDeviceBaseModel) to
  trigger on-demand installation for use cases requiring a base model.
  If the download fails, the UI will be stuck in the downloading state
  until the next CUS scheduled update.
- Plumb ModelStatus in OnDeviceAiSettingsHandler

[iOS][APC] Deflake and re-enable DesktopLayoutPageAtRestAndZoomedMatchesVisualViewport

WaitForPresentationUpdate() (TakeSnapshot) was called inside
WaitUntilConditionOrTimeout polling loops in ZoomPageToScale() before
WebKit settled the zoom transition. On desktop-layout pages, taking
snapshots mid-zoom caused WebKit to revert UIScrollView.zoomScale back
to minimumZoomScale, causing thrashing until the 10s timeout expired.

- Move WaitForPresentationUpdate() outside the polling loops so it runs
  once after the zoom scale and scroll offset settle.
- Evaluate window.visualViewport properties before reading ZoomScale()
  so any scale reversion during the JS eval run-loop spin is caught and
  re-applied.
- Re-enable DesktopLayoutPageAtRestAndZoomedMatchesVisualViewport.

Verified via UTR (`ios_chrome_unittests iPhone 16 27.0`) with 50
repeated runs and 50/50 passes

TAG=agy

Reland "[tabstrip_ntb] Push initial OS theme settings before profile creation"

This is a reland of commit d1609605b6c29d9fa792683247cdf69f35b05146

The original CL caused NightModeEnabled render tests in
SigninPromoCoordinatorTest to produce light mode images. Reading the
night mode in PreProfileInit creates the global night mode controller,
which applies AppCompat's default night mode from the theme setting
preference. In production, Chrome activities create that controller
before native initialization, so the behavior is unchanged. The test,
however, set night mode only through AppCompatDelegate and started the
browser process directly, so the controller was first created in
PreProfileInit and reset the default night mode to light before the test
activity launched.

Original change's description:
> [tabstrip_ntb] Push initial OS theme settings before profile creation
>
> The NTB WebUI attaches its WebContents to the activity's WindowAndroid
> during profile creation, but the initial night mode and system accent
> color were only pushed to native in post-native init. Each push reset
> the ColorProvider cache, so the NTB built its ColorProviders three times
> during startup.
>
> When the NTB WebUI is enabled, push the initial values from
> PreProfileInit, before any WebContents uses the cache, so the later push
> in post-native init is a no-op. Also add a trace event to
> WebContentsImpl::HandleColorRelatedStateChanges to make this cost
> visible in startup traces.
>
> Bug: 581783426

[Vertical Tabs] Restore tabs on exit from content fullscreen

When exiting content fullscreen on mac via the window's green fullscreen
button, AppKit notifies BrowserView::FullscreenStateChanging() (which
runs PrepareFullscreen() / relayout) before FullscreenStateChanged()
notifies FullscreenController. Because FullscreenController had not yet
cleared tab-fullscreen state, BrowserView still treated the window as
being in content fullscreen during the exit transition, hiding chrome UI
such as the vertical tab strip until the transition finished.

Add FullscreenController::WindowFullscreenStateChanging() and invoke it
from BrowserView::FullscreenStateChanging() on macOS so tab fullscreen
state is cleared as soon as the window begins exiting fullscreen.

Migrate l10n_util::GetDisplayNameForCountry to take a LanguageTag

Change the `display_locale` parameter of GetDisplayNameForCountry() from
std::string_view to base::i18n::LanguageTag, for both the ICU and iOS
implementations. An "und" display locale now plays the role an empty
string used to and yields an empty result.

CountryNamesForLocale passes its LanguageTag through directly; other
callers parse their locale string at the call site.

Bug: None

CCT: Restore saved state in BaseCustomTabRootUiCoordinator

A cross-display requestFullscreen() moves the window to the other
display, which recreates the activity. RootUiCoordinator restores the
fullscreen options that the outgoing activity saved, so that the new
activity re-enters fullscreen on the new display. ChromeTabbedActivity
passes its saved instance state to RootUiCoordinator, but
BaseCustomTabRootUiCoordinator passed null. A Custom Tab therefore lost
HTML fullscreen when its tab was re-attached to the recreated activity,
and never re-entered it.

Pass getSavedInstanceState() and getPersistentInstanceState() through.
The rest of the save/restore cycle is shared with ChromeTabbedActivity
and already runs for Custom Tabs. History: the saved-state input to
RootUiCoordinator predates this code; 318d0b33974fa changed it from a
Supplier<Bundle> to a Bundle, c75a96efda7d9 added persistentState, and
c7095f282c4a2 reused the bundle for the fullscreen restore without
updating the Custom Tab call site. getPersistentInstanceState() is
always null outside ChromeTabbedActivity and is passed for symmetry.

Saved fullscreen options exist only after a cross-display request (the
only path that does not exit fullscreen on stop), so other recreations
are unaffected by the fullscreen part of this change.

Side effect: RootUiCoordinator also restores the incognito re-auth
pending flag from the same bundle. An off-the-record Custom Tab with
Incognito lock enabled now shows the full-screen re-auth page after an
activity recreation from saved state, including foreground recreations
(display move, density change, dark-mode toggle) and restore after
process death. Before, the saved flag was discarded and the page came
back without the re-auth page. Regular Custom Tabs are not affected.
Custom Tabs have no IncognitoRestoreAppLaunchDrawBlocker, so page
content may be visible briefly before the re-auth page.

stays.

[Extensions] Add browsertests for iframe CSP frame-ancestors behavior

Before this CL, ExtensionIframeAntiFramingBrowserTest only covered the
X-Frame-Options HTTP header across extensions and embedded frames,
leaving Content-Security-Policy: frame-ancestors enforcement untested
across nested web and multi-extension hierarchies.

After this CL, automated browsertests also verify that
content::AncestorThrottle blocks embedding when HTTP responses include
Content-Security-Policy: frame-ancestors directives that exclude any
extension or web ancestor in the frame tree, including overriding
X-Frame-Options: ALLOWALL.

[Cast Streaming] Report frame playout events in StreamConsumer

Report frame playout events to openscreen::cast::Receiver when frames
are consumed and forwarded by StreamConsumer::MaybeSendNextFrame(). This
enables RTCP kFramePlayedOut receiver event logs so the Cast Streaming
sender can compute end-to-end latency and frame lateness statistics.

Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Mon Nov 16 22:18:34 2026
tree
f613e92986e8aa9a0070f29aa9c4a20df8d752ca
parent
a55c940aa24ca2239bc97466601724c06b00220e [ diff ] 
[omnibox] Keep the Full WebUI popup over a location bar moved by layout

The Full WebUI omnibox popup is a separate widget that is kept over the
location bar. Its bounds are re-synced when LocationBarView notifies its
LocationBar::Observers from OnBoundsChanged(), and when the browser
widget moves or resizes. Nothing re-synced them when an ancestor's
layout moved the location bar within the browser widget without changing
the location bar's own bounds.

That happens when resizing the vertical tab strip in a narrow window.
The toolbar moves with the tab strip's edge, but the location bar is at
its minimum width, so the toolbar's other children (e.g. the profile
chip's label) absorb the width change. The location bar's bounds
relative to the toolbar don't change, so OnBoundsChanged() isn't called
and the popup is left at its old position, offset from the location bar
it's meant to cover.

Views does call LocationBarView::OnVisibleBoundsChanged() whenever an
ancestor's bounds change, but it only called UpdatePopupAppearance(),
which is an intentional no-op for the Full popup. It now also notifies
the LocationBar::Observers when the location bar's bounds in the widget
changed since the last notification. That covers any ancestor move, not
just the vertical tab strip, and the check skips the notification when
OnBoundsChanged() already sent one, or when the ancestor's change didn't
move the location bar.

BEFORE/AFTER:
http://screencast/cast/NjY5Mzg4MDQyNTYxMTI2NHw5Zjk5NTI2Yy0wMg

20b957440ffc1b099dc153f8fbeb83bceda3bc08
parent
8d3c0bc852bc37406d9c9227e833b7db45f99992 [ diff ] 
EAM: Don't crash on a pending fullscreen without a current tab

ExclusiveAccessManager.initialize() re-enters fullscreen that was saved
before an activity recreation. It assumed that the tab model always has
a current tab at that point and asserted it, then dereferenced the tab.

That holds for ChromeTabbedActivity, and today Custom Tabs never reach
this code because BaseCustomTabRootUiCoordinator does not pass the saved
instance state (fixed in a follow-up CL). Once it does, an off-the-record
Custom Tab (incognito or ephemeral) restored from a bundle that holds
the cipher key defers adding its tab to the model until
CookiesFetcher.restoreCookies() finishes on an AsyncTask, while
initialize() runs in the same call stack as finishNativeInitialization().
The current tab is then null: an assertion failure in debug builds and
a NullPointerException in release builds.

When there is no current tab, skip the re-enter, clear the pending
state and drop the saved fullscreen options. Keeping the options would
de-duplicate the page's next identical fullscreen request in
enterFullscreenModeForTab() and save them again into the next bundle.
The existing branch for a tab without WebContents is unchanged.

LATEST_FULLSCREEN_OPTIONS is made visible for tests.

[Extensions] Add browsertests for iframe X-Frame-Options behavior

Before this CL, no end-to-end browsertests verified how the
X-Frame-Options HTTP header behaves across extensions and embedded
frames. As a result, regressions in anti-framing enforcement for
extension documents and web frames embedded by extensions could go
undetected.

After this CL, automated browsertests cover the current X-Frame-Options
behavior for extensions. The tests verify that content::AncestorThrottle
blocks embedding when HTTP responses include X-Frame-Options: DENY and
X-Frame-Options: SAMEORIGIN headers.

The tests are modeled after the scenarios in this (google internal)
document [1] (in the "Proposal" section). Content-Security-Policy:
frame-ancestors scenarios are covered in a follow-up CL.

[1]:
https://docs.google.com/document/d/1A4JgEGJguCVi6-2L2fKWh3yfqh1EtiQMSsa4k0nHpLE/edit?tab=t.0#heading=h.k95bj1u1xhq0

ff5d2c210707c40033a0f573432b6b95b6648e7a [ diff ] 
[🌷] Fix ViewTransitionPixelTest animation and controls flakes

testDialog and testWiderThanIcb called startTransitionAnimation() and
immediately invoked animateToEndState() without awaiting
transition.ready via waitForTransitionReady(). Because ViewTransition
pseudo-elements and animations are generated asynchronously by Blink,
advancing animations to the end state before the transition was ready
caused animations to not apply to the incoming elements, resulting in
mid-transition screenshots missing the dialog or wider element.

Additionally, testBrowserControlsRootSnapshotControlsOverlay and
testBrowserControlsChildSnapshotControlsOverlay hid browser controls
before navigation, but on navigation to the incoming page, only
waited for presentation frames without waiting for the browser
controls animation to settle to fully shown (offset 0). Call
waitForBrowserControlsState(true) before capturing screenshots to
prevent capturing in-flight controls animations.

UTR: 63/63 passed on android-x64-rel (chrome_public_test_apk,
*ViewTransitionPixelTest*) —
https://chromium-swarm.appspot.com/task?id=7bf2ee0f09716410

tree
31b3736ff4d4dd1bcd819c1d86a9468470b3136f
parent
93cb814e10d41fa3b2cc47c9af1208753884aa31 [ diff ] 
activity_reporter: exclude per-user Azure AD workplace join on Windows

On Windows, ActivityReporterConfigurator::IsMachineExternallyManaged()
currently calls
PlatformManagementService::GetManagementAuthorityTrustworthiness(),
which includes AzureActiveDirectoryStatusProvider
(base::win::IsJoinedToAzureAD()). Because Chrome runs as the interactive
user, NetGetAadJoinInformation() sees per-user DSREG_WORKPLACE_JOIN
entries in HKCU (personal devices with a linked work/school account),
whereas the system-level updater service runs as NT AUTHORITY\SYSTEM and
only sees machine-level DSREG_DEVICE_JOIN entries in HKLM.

Switching to GetManagementAuthorityTrustworthinessForPolicyLoading()
skips kAzureActiveDirectoryManagement on Windows so that only
AzureActiveDirectoryDeviceStatusProvider
(base::win::IsDeviceJoinedToAzureAD()) contributes CLOUD_DOMAIN, while
remaining identical to GetManagementAuthorityTrustworthiness() on all
non-Windows platforms and leaving PlatformManagementService unchanged
for other callers.

Migrate TabAlertController::active_alerts_ to base::EnumSet

Replaces the per-tab base::flat_set<TabAlert, CompareAlerts> with a
4-byte inline base::EnumSet<TabAlert> to eliminate heap allocations and
sorted-vector comparator overhead on every open tab.

- Add kMinValue and kMaxValue bounds to tabs::TabAlert so it can be
  stored directly in base::EnumSet<TabAlert>.
- Replace CompareAlerts and its fixed_flat_map lookup with a constexpr
  kTabAlertPriority array and a compile-time check ensuring every
  non-kNone alert is listed once.
- Update TabAlertController queries and mutations to use EnumSet
  bitmask operations and scan kTabAlertPriority only when alerts are
  active.

NO_IFTTT=Only adding kMinValue/kMaxValue aliases and replacing CompareAlerts with a priority array without changing alert states or priority order.

TAG=agy
CONV=bef564c7-87eb-4c78-b84e-a0097abcf6c3

Fullscreen: Don't retain the activity through the fullscreen mode callback

FullscreenHtmlApiHandlerBase passes an OutcomeReceiver to
Activity#requestFullscreenMode() when it enters HTML fullscreen and when
it returns a window from another display. Both were non-static: an
anonymous class and a lambda that capture the handler, which holds the
activity.

The framework wraps the receiver in an IRemoteCallback.Stub and sends it
to system_server. A binder stub stays reachable in the app process, as a
native global reference, until the remote side releases it. Until then
the stub keeps the receiver, the handler and the destroyed activity
alive. LeakCanary reports this as:

  GC Root: Global variable in native code
  android.app.FullscreenRequestHandler$1.val$approvalCallback
  FullscreenHtmlApiHandlerBase$3.this$0
  FullscreenHtmlApiHandlerBase.mActivity
  CustomTabActivity (destroyed)

Make both receivers static nested classes that hold the handler through
a WeakReference, as FullscreenHandler in the same file already does. The
stub can still outlive the activity, but it no longer reaches it. A
result that arrives after the handler is gone is dropped; it only
updated handler state.

e6d1cacd535686e6cf52a8ba7473527e63f89173
parent
6290198a7658ff3fe37b17209a0cbc69a1db6a77 [ diff ] 
[Page Actions] Animate out anchored messages in WebUI toolbar

This CL ports the anchored message exit animation from Views
(`PageActionView`, crrev.com/c/8349189) to `WebUIPageActionControl` so
anchored messages fade out smoothly instead of closing immediately.

While animating out, `IsAnchoredMessageVisible()` returns false so icon
ordering updates immediately. When `HideAnchoredMessage()` dismisses
only the bubble while the icon remains visible, clicking the icon during
fade-out invokes its action rather than downgrading a closing bubble to
a chip. If `ShowAnchoredMessage()` is called again programmatically
while closing, `AnimateIn()` reverses the exit animation once the WebUI
anchor element is available.

Also added unit tests for exit animation, deferred anchors, and reduced
motion, and updated the browser test to verify fade-out and anchoring.

tree
b3a5d22bb7d48f9d3b0b2205d2c2bb914929d7bd
parent
3a615367cc5b412966488354b8f38c4f4bbf45da [ diff ] 
[glic] Handle the panel hotkey locally without global hotkey support

On Wayland, GlicBackgroundModeManager can't register the OS-global
panel toggle hotkey (Alt+G): either there is no
ui::GlobalAcceleratorListener at all (Ozone/Wayland doesn't implement
one, e.g. GNOME without the GlobalShortcuts portal), or the listener is
the GlobalShortcuts portal one, which only registers shortcuts from
extension commands (IsRegistrationHandledExternally()) and rejects
RegisterAccelerator(). InstanceIndependentHotkeyManager still passes
the hotkey through because it considers it globally scoped, so the
hotkey does nothing at all.

Treat the panel toggle hotkey as locally scoped when OS-global hotkeys
aren't supported, so it works while a Chrome window is focused, like
the local scope already does. Tests that expect the hotkey to be passed
through are skipped in that configuration, and the new tests only run
there (e.g. on the Wayland bots).

cc: Integrate ViewTransitionSnapshotState into Scheduler (2/4)

Update SchedulerStateMachine and Scheduler to consult
ViewTransitionSnapshotState while a cross-document ViewTransition
snapshot kSave directive is in flight:
- Move pause_rendering_ and
  waiting_for_activation_after_rendering_resumed_ into
  ViewTransitionSnapshotState.
- Suppress new BeginMainFrames and impl-side invalidations while kSave
  is capturing.
- Do not force-activate an unrasterized kSave pending tree when the
  external BeginFrameSource is paused.
- Allow the activated kSave tree to draw even when the external
  BeginFrameSource is paused or a previous frame ack is pending.
- Issue a manual BeginFrame when the kSave pending tree activates while
  BeginImplFrameState is IDLE.
- Automatically clear the capturing state when the active tree draws,
  aborts drawing, or loses its LayerTreeFrameSink.

This is part 2 of a 4-CL series.

b3205263d10d107a40ca6956600d6e6e212b37b1 [ diff ] 
Remove resource.url from pending allowlist in OnBlockingPageDone

BaseUIManager::DisplayBlockingPage inserts `resource.url` into the tab's
pending AllowlistUrlSet (`pending_allowlisted_entries_`), whereas
BaseUIManager::OnBlockingPageDone previously only updated or removed
`main_frame_url`.

When `resource.url` and `main_frame_url` map to different allowlist keys
(for example, when a cross-host redirect chain triggers an interstitial
or when `resource.url` is a `view-source:` URL), `resource.url` remained
leaked in `pending_allowlisted_entries_`.

Ensure OnBlockingPageDone removes `resource.url` from the pending
allowlist on both proceed (when its allowlist key differs from
`main_frame_url`) and non-proceed (removing `resource.url` and, if its
key differs, `main_frame_url`), while guarding `GetAllowlistEntryKey`
against empty or invalid URLs passed by embedders before commit.

71c9b33fabb249fa326575f67f2d472eeec16e66 [ diff ] 
[PH] Gate WebView AllowNonActivatedCrossOriginPaintHolding behind a flag

WebView was the only ContentBrowserClient overriding
AllowNonActivatedCrossOriginPaintHolding() to return true. This
exemption was originally added before initial empty document paint
holding and asynchronous ForceFirstFrameAfterNavigationTimeout() landed.

Put WebView's AllowNonActivatedCrossOriginPaintHolding() behavior behind
a killswitch flag (kWebViewLimitCrossOriginNonActivatedPaintHolding,
enabled by default) so WebView follows the same cross-origin
non-activated paint holding restriction as other embedders while
retaining a Finch killswitch.

Add UMA histograms for enterprise extension actor attribution

Extension actor attribution (kEnterpriseExtensionActorAttribution) tags
enterprise security events with the extension that drove a tab or
started a download, but there is currently no visibility into how often
events are attributed or why attribution is lost in the field. This adds
three histograms under Enterprise.ExtensionActorAttribution.*:

- Lookup.WebContents.Result: whether a tab had an active extension
  actor when one was looked up for an enterprise security event or a
  real-time URL lookup.
- Lookup.Download.Result: the strongest extension signal on a download:
  the chrome.downloads API, a chrome-extension:// request initiator, an
  active extension actor on the originating tab, or none. Only the
  chrome.downloads signal attributes the download today; the other two
  size the downloads that bypass it (crbug.com/570065942).
- Tracker.Cleared: why an active actor was reset (user interaction,
  browser-initiated navigation, non-extension navigation, explicit
  clear). Only recorded when an actor was actually present.

Nothing is recorded while the feature is disabled.

The tracker's four reset sites now go through a single
ResetActiveActor() helper so that every clear is recorded exactly once.

6183a7ed47e73431291efe6f94e2712d6a9fe7ee
parent
1b6fa2aad2bc2e66aef134d6c6b8397b18a162f8 [ diff ] 
Add opacity: auto

Overlapping highlight pseudos choose a single winning value for text
color by finding the topmost highlight that has a value for the color
property that is not currentColor. The opacity property has no similar
sentinel value. We introduce the auto keyword for that purpose. In
contexts other than a highlight pseudo, auto computes to 1. Subsequent
changes will compute auto to itself on highlight pseudos and use it to
determine the winning opacity value.

parent
d11f5a96bf447a7b2dc8fe7a8f84a2427c01903d [ diff ] 
Stop using OnWidgetClosing in OmniboxEverywhereRegionSelectOverlay

WidgetObserver::OnWidgetClosing() and OnWidgetDestroying() are
deprecated (crbug.com/40194222). The overlay used them to cancel the
selection in unison when one of its widgets was closed by something
other than the overlay itself (e.g. WM_CLOSE on Windows ->
Widget::Close(), CloseNow(), or the platform destroying the native
window on display disconnect).

Replace them with Widget::MakeCloseSynchronous(), the intended
successor. Views runs the installed callback synchronously from
CloseWithReason() and from OnNativeWidgetDestroyed(), so a single hook
covers every path and the overlay no longer needs to be a WidgetObserver
at all.

Because the override intercepts Widget::Close(), Finish() now tears its
widgets down with CloseNow() rather than Close(). That also keeps the
whole close sequence synchronous: by the time the completion callback
runs, every widget is closed, sibling overlays on other displays are
gone, and OmniboxEverywhereUIManager::HasOpenModalDialog() is already
false, exactly as with the old OnWidgetClosing() path.

Tighten the multi-display close tests to assert the callback has run
synchronously (IsReady() instead of Wait()), and add a regression test
for the CloseNow() case.

93119c5126eea775fb879db1efee0c1f9e6fcc3b [ diff ] 
Redact no-CORS script frames in Reporting API crash stacks

The existing filter redacted stack frames for scripts with extension
URLs but failed to redact cross-origin scripts loaded without CORS.

Use the origin-aware overload of v8::Message::PrintCurrentStackTrace,
which allows the filter to redact frames from scripts that are not
shared cross-origin. Frames without a script, such as built-in
functions, are filtered using the nearest caller with a script, or
redacted if there is no such caller.

Add browser tests for same-origin, CORS, no-CORS and redirected scripts,
and unit tests for the collector's filter.

[SBER Deprecation] Remove obsolete Extended Reporting toggle tests

SBER deprecation (kExtendedReportingRemovePrefDependency) has been
enabled by default on Chrome for a while now, so the settings tests that
force it off are no longer covering anything users can hit.

This drops the five FlagsDisabled tests that were left behind with a
TODO pointing at this bug, plus the SafeBrowsingLabelsAndToggles case
that checks the "not deprecated" state. FlagsDisabled also no longer
needs to override extendedReportingRemovePrefDependency.

The "deprecated + HPRT not sampled" test stays, since the page still
shows the toggle when the sample-ping kill switch is off.

Reland "Return actual counts in WebFeatureHistogramTester::WaitForCountsAtLeast"

This is a reland of commit 126805d072ee550d8f936e08087c2652bd7b354f

The original change was reverted because
WebAppTitleBrowserTest.AppTitleNavigation failed on linux-bfcache-rel,
which runs browser_tests with `--disable-features=BackForwardCache`.
The test navigates back to a page with an app title. Without the
back/forward cache, that page is loaded again and records
`kWebAppTitle` a second time, which the old `WaitForCountsAtLeast()`
masked. The test now expects 1 count when the back navigation is served
from the back/forward cache and 2 otherwise.

Original change's description:
> Return actual counts in WebFeatureHistogramTester::WaitForCountsAtLeast
>
> WebFeatureHistogramTester::WaitForCountsAtLeast() is documented to
> return the last observed feature counts, but ended with
> `return expected;`. As a result, `ExpectCounts()` always passed once
> the wait loop exited, masking over-counts or unexpected non-zero counts
> on zero-expected features.
>
> Return `counts` instead of `expected`, add browser tests covering
> matching counts, over-counts, and unexpected feature counts, and update
> three stale `LocalNetworkAccessCountersBrowserTest` expectations that
> navigate from a public HTTPS page to a loopback server and record
> `kAddressSpacePublicSecureContextNavigatedToLoopbackV2`.
>
> Fixed: 585276537

Revert "[🌷] Disable ThreadControllerWithMessagePumpTest.EarlyQuit on Android"

This reverts commit 438f05e08043eacddca61129f64fb048683b4e1a.

Reason for revert:
Disabling EarlyQuit shifted the leaked g_run_tasks_by_batches failure
onto ThreadControllerWithMessagePumpTest.ThreadControllerActiveAdvancedNesting
(14/14 builds across 6 Android builders). Re-enabling EarlyQuit and
fixing the state leak instead.
Sample build: https://ci.chromium.org/ui/p/chromium/builders/ci/android-12l-x64-dbg-tests/45716/overview

Original change's description:
> [🌷] Disable ThreadControllerWithMessagePumpTest.EarlyQuit on Android
>
> EarlyQuit fails its first attempt in 100% of builds on Android (chrome
> android-arm(64)-tests, automotive, official testers, chromium
> ToTAndroid) since 2026-11-06 and passes on retry. base/i18n tests call
> features::Init(), which leaves g_run_tasks_by_batches false on Android
> (kRunTasksByBatches is disabled there). The fixture only resets it in
> TearDown, so EarlyQuit runs only one task per DoWork() when it is the
> first test of its fixture in a batch. Only Android is affected.
>
> Test history: https://ci.chromium.org/ui/test/chrome/%3A%2F%2Fbase%5C%3Abase_unittests%21gtest%3A%3AThreadControllerWithMessagePumpTest%23EarlyQuit
> Failed build: https://ci.chromium.org/b/8665217707320840785
>

a4210613e71062895bc2ec0abc74dfba1bd81576 [ diff ] 
[Feed] Move the Android desktop feed check to FeedService::IsEnabled()

The feed is turned off on Android desktop only in Java
(FeedFeatures.isFeedEnabled()), so native code that only checks
FeedService::IsEnabled() doesn't know about it. For example, background
refreshes keep fetching the feed on desktop devices that showed it
before, even though it can't be shown.

In this CL:
- Turn off the feed on Android desktop in FeedService::IsEnabled()
  instead of FeedFeatures.isFeedEnabled(). The NTP still hides the feed
  (through FeedServiceBridge), and native feed loads, including
  background refreshes, are now blocked on desktop too.

Other effects on Android desktop: feed data stored before is cleared
once at the next startup (as when the feed is turned off by policy),
feed-internals shows the feed as not allowed, and
ContentSuggestions.Feed.UserSettingsOnStart reports kFeedNotEnabled (not
kFeedNotEnabledByPolicy).

This prepares for turning off the feed on cars in the same place.

Bug: 337813022
Test: tools/autotest.py -C out/Default components/feed/core/v2/public/feed_service_unittest.cc

parent
90add2f678d6c16d85e71458e60a36665b5e3b78 [ diff ] 
[iOS][Actor] Post tool-initiated task interrupts

ActorEngine posts InterruptFromTool and UninterruptFromTool on the
current sequence so a tool never re-enters ActorTask or ActorEngine on
its own stack. No tool re-enters today, since pause/resume is not
implemented yet; this keeps it that way once it is. Posted calls are
dropped unless the tool that issued them is still executing.

ToolDelegate now documents the contract: methods that change task state
are posted, while queries and registration (IsWindowIdValid,
InsertWebState) stay synchronous as they must return a result.

ActorTaskFormFillingHandler now posts intervention delegate completions,
matching ActorTask, and runs the tool callbacks directly from them.

Fixed: 568686304

edbdd141389f8b56fed8e15a1ba55379d5b655f5 [ diff ] 
[omnibox][webui] Don't add .com on ctrl+enter if ctrl pressed elsewhere

Ctrl+enter adds 'www....com' to the input.

But to avoid accidental usage, it only applies if ctrl isn't already
consumed by other shortcuts like ctrl+L or ctrl+v.

WebUI omnibox could not see ctrl consumption that happened in the
webpage. E.g. webpage is focused and user presses ctrl+L. The omnibox
just sees that ctrl is still held down and knows it hasn't been consumed
yet in the omnibox.

This CL makes the omnibox ignore ctrl unless it was pressed down while
the omnibox itself was focused. Thus avoiding already-consumed ctrls
sneaking into the omnibox.

Bug: 584443534

Draw images at whole device pixels without pixel canvas recording
📋 Metadata
commit
523c75bfa49bdb0d3fe69d12f1ca8a418d799a03
[ log ]
author

Mon Nov 16 21:42:48 2026
committer

Mon Nov 16 21:42:48 2026
tree
fe7e2e06784a9d1ce689d73390cf24408a6f9f76
parent
6032a6f255688a774bd32797f7b39424e1fc2bfa [ diff ] 
Draw images at whole device pixels without pixel canvas recording

Without pixel canvas recording (every platform except ChromeOS), views
record in DIPs and cc rasters them at the device scale factor, so a
view at an integer DIP offset inside its layer can land at a fractional
device pixel at fractional scales, e.g. the 10 DIP client-side frame
inset on Linux/Wayland lands at 12.5 px at 1.25x. Images drawn there
are sampled at half-pixel offsets: with nearest sampling (bookmark bar
favicons, ImageView) GPUs drop and double rows, and with bilinear
sampling (tab favicons since crrev.com/c/7213840) they get blurred.

ui::PaintRecorder now tells gfx::Canvas the offset of the view from its
(pixel-aligned) layer, and gfx::Canvas::DrawImageInt() moves images by
less than a pixel so they start on a whole device pixel. ui::PaintCache
only reuses a record at an offset that differs by whole device pixels.

[iOS] Host ActuationWorklogView in a self-sizing cell in the AI sandbox.

Updates the AI prototyping worklog sandbox to host ActuationWorklogView
inside a collection view cell that replicates the Gemini SDK custom
content cell layout and height invalidation contract, followed by a
mock sibling response block.

This allows manually verifying that the worklog self-sizes and that
sibling blocks reflow when it collapses, expands, adds steps or toggles
its chip. Prototyping-only code; not shipped.

[glic] Extend expiry of Glic.Instance.Session.* histograms

Extends the expiration date to 2027-11-16 for four session metric
entries in glic/histograms.xml:
- Glic{GlicProvider}.Instance.Session.CreateTabCount
- Glic{GlicProvider}.Instance.Session.TotalInactiveDuration
- Glic.Instance.Session.UninterruptedActiveDuration
- Glic.Instance.Session.UninterruptedVisibleDuration

CreateTabCount and TotalInactiveDuration are actively tracked on the
GEiC metrics dashboard, and the uninterrupted session duration metrics
remain active alongside their instance-level counterparts.

[iOS] Make ActuationWorklogView self-sizing and self-collapsing.

Prepares ActuationWorklogView to be hosted directly inside a self-sizing
collection view cell, such as the Gemini SDK custom content cell. The
view now hugs its vertical content so a host cell sizes to it instead of
stretching it.

The view also handles taps on its header item to toggle its collapsed
state, which is now exposed, and forwards accessory taps through its own
delegate so hosts no longer depend on item views. A follow-up CL
exercises this in the AI prototyping sandbox.

tree
d476c476a5a530f06eda68930acfcf5314a394f3
parent
ce2d584ce8510d2818f1466bd55a0e3dcc2c3d96 [ diff ] 
[Perf] Replace boxed Map with SparseArray in OfflinePageTabObserver

In OfflinePageTabObserver, mObservedTabs is a primitive-keyed map
(Map<Integer, TabState>).

What changes:
- Converts Map<Integer, TabState> declarations to
  android.util.SparseArray.
- Rewrites iteration over entrySet/keySet/values to indexed loops.
- Rewrites remove(k) to delete(k), containsKey(k) to indexOfKey(k) >=
  0, and isEmpty() to size() == 0 / size() != 0.

Why it saves memory and CPU:
- Avoids Integer wrapper object boxing for primitive keys.
- Avoids wrapper object boxing and HashMap.Node allocations (~32-48
  bytes per entry).

Why behavior is unchanged:
- Map reference does not escape the declaring class or package.
- Local construction ensures all entries originate within the class.
- Loop bodies do not depend on unspecified HashMap iteration order.

Why:
mObservedTabs is a Map<Integer, TabState>, which boxes every key into
an object.

parent
24c43b5e3ea539d800ca5265218b7dad4fb669b4 [ diff ] 
[Java TabGroupId] Introduce Java TabGroupId

The C++ `TabGroupId` is represented by its underlying raw `Token` in
Java (see an example at [1]). This lacks clarity and will be error-prone
if the future Java `SplitTabId` follows the same pattern, since
`SplitTabId` is also backed by a `Token`.

To fix the issue, this CL introduces a Java `TabGroupId` as the C++
`TabGroupId`'s counterpart. Subsequent CLs will migrate existing `Token`
usages to the Java `TabGroupId`.

To mirror the C++ architecture, we'll also introduce an abstract
`TokenId` class as the base class for `TabGroupId` and the future
`SplitTabId`.

* C++ : TokenId (template)
* Java: TokenId (abstract class)

* C++: TabGroupId (TokenId<TabGroupId>)
* Java: TabGroupId (extends TokenId)

[1] https://source.chromium.org/chromium/chromium/src/+/main:chrome/browser/tab/java/src/org/chromium/chrome/browser/tab/Tab.java;l=466;drc=867df0ff70b91b9f5d82f399ecce8dd973fe1c00

[Glic] Attach DeviceCapabilities on session start and first response

Sync DeviceInfo can be stale when a device wakes from FCM or lacks
an eligible browser window or visible opt-in surface at runtime.
Sending DeviceCapabilities upon StartSession FCM wake-up and on the
first outgoing GlicExperimentalTriggering response in a transport
session allows Chrome to report its live experimental triggering
state, capabilities, browser window availability, and opt-in UI
eligibility on the initial round-trip without extra requests.

[🌷] Thread-safe access to Flag.sFlagsCreatedForTesting

Synchronize access to Flag.sFlagsCreatedForTesting using a dedicated
lock object (sFlagsCreatedLock) and @GuardedBy("sFlagsCreatedLock").
This prevents ConcurrentModificationException when background threads
create or register Flag instances concurrently during test teardown and
flag cache clearing in batched tests.

*WebsitePreferenceBridgeTest*) —

commit
fa92774e00d28878e1c8df388eec14552b755b3f

tree
72283036cf9bb3bc99e3b5abbd2267426c12635d
parent
569fceabc791860fbd848b7da0626e0e94ae52b7 [ diff ] 
[Transit] Make Outcome<R>, the state-changes half of a Trip, public

A Trip has two halves: the Trigger (what to do, plus options like
timeouts) and the state changes it causes (the Station to arrive at,
the Facilities and States to enter or exit, the Conditions to wait
for). Only the Trigger half can be built on its own today, as a
TripBuilder returned by xxxTo(). A helper that knows the end state
must therefore also own the Trigger and run the Trip, so callers get
"...And()" twins that hide the destination inside a TripBuilder,
results fetched back with Trip.get(Class), and Pair returns.

Make Outcome, which the parent CL extracted from TripBuilder, public
as Outcome<R>: a typed, one-shot set of state changes with static
factories. TripBuilder.reach(Outcome<R>) merges it into the Trip's own
Outcome, runs the Trip and returns R.
Outcome.after(TripBuilder) does the same join from the other side, for
call sites where the end state is the point and the Trigger is long or
passed in. TripBuilder is unchanged, so a Station can offer the end
state while the caller keeps choosing the Trigger and its options.

Use it where the destination was hidden inside a TripBuilder:
- Journeys.beginNewTabGroupUiFlow(TripBuilder) becomes the
  newTabGroupUiFlow() Outcome, reached from four different Triggers.
- HubBaseStation pane selection no longer needs Trip.get(Class).
- TabListEditorAppMenu.groupTabsWithoutDialog() returns a record via
  Outcome.returning() instead of a Pair.

Also replace CtaPageStation.open(Regular|Incognito)TabSwitcherAnd(),
whose callers fetched the Station back with completeAndGet(), with a
clickTabSwitcherButtonTo() Trigger; TabSwitcherLayoutPTTest chains its
thumbnail State onto it and arrives at the tab switcher directly.

Revert "[ios] Align BadgeTypeForInfobarType with IsBadgeSupportedForInfobarType"

This reverts commit 47bfce807d3191b6fc6a0d0d25e56d2398e96bb3.

Reason for revert: The settings (gear) button on the Save Password,
Update Password, Save Card and Save Address infobar banners
disappeared. InfobarOverlayRequestConfig::has_badge() is derived from
BadgeTypeForInfobarType() and drives the banner's presentsModal, so
mapping these infobars to kBadgeTypeNone also removed the button that
opens their modal.

Reverting is safe: IsBadgeSupportedForInfobarType() still returns false
for these infobars, so they never become location bar badges.

Original change's description:
> [ios] Align BadgeTypeForInfobarType with IsBadgeSupportedForInfobarType

> permanently do not display location bar badges. Previously,
> BadgeTypeForInfobarType still returned obsolete badge types (such as
> kBadgeTypePasswordSave) for these infobars, while
> IsBadgeSupportedForInfobarType returned false. Callers querying
> BadgeTypeForInfobarType (such as InfobarOverlayRequestConfig or
> BadgeMediator) diverged from IsBadgeSupportedForInfobarType.

[iOS][Passwords] Remove IdentityManager & PrefService from PMVC

Move PrefService-backed `allowsAddPassword` evaluation into
`PasswordsMediator` and expose it via `PasswordsConsumer`, and remove
`IdentityManagerObserverBridge` from `PasswordManagerViewController`.
`primaryAccountDidChange:` previously only triggered a redundant `[self
reloadData]` with unchanged VC state, while actual account and sync
state updates are already driven by `PasswordsMediator` via
`PasswordsConsumer`.

This removes C++ service dependencies from the view controller,
simplifies its initializer to `-initWithShouldOpenInSearchMode:`, and
cleans up `:ui` BUILD dependencies.

tree
89f1c781ce2d567d44fd4ac6046d4672e69dee62
parent
58757435b32306796da1f8065d6c3ff94fcdf824 [ diff ] 
exo: Log warning when an unhandled key ack arrives after expiration

Keyboard::ProcessExpiredPendingKeyAcks() previously erased expired
entries from pending_key_acks_ silently, and Keyboard::AckKeyboardKey()
returned without logging when an acknowledgment arrived after the 1000ms
timeout had already expired. Because Wayland clients may omit ack_key
for handled keys and send ack_key when keys are unhandled or dropped,
late acknowledgments caused unhandled accelerators (such as Ctrl+N from
ARC) to be silently dropped with no diagnostic signal in chrome.log.

Retain unhandled expired entries in a bounded LRU cache (cleared on
focus changes) and log a warning with non-sensitive event metadata and
elapsed latency when AckKeyboardKey() receives a late unhandled ack.

TAG=agy
CONV=da644b03-fad8-4290-ab38-e036e48d7567

Harden Widget::MakeCloseSynchronous against re-entrancy and cancellation
📋 Metadata

Foromo Daniel Soromou <koretadaniel@chromium.org>

Mon Nov 16 21:15:47 2026
tree
c019f0a8c5dee463f205298af1acf4c9d9018abb
parent
7b49840f309456972ee110180d7ce97bc3ffdcf3 [ diff ] 
Harden Widget::MakeCloseSynchronous against re-entrancy and cancellation

Fix four lifecycle edge cases in Widget's override_close_ handling so
callers of MakeCloseSynchronous() do not need to manually clear the
callback before closing or destroying the widget:

1. Check widget_closed_ before override_close_ in CloseWithReason() so
   re-entrant Close() calls during CloseNow(),
   OnNativeWidgetDestroyed(), or ~Widget() early-return instead of
   firing override_close_ mid- teardown.
2. Reset override_close_ on ~Widget() entry so synchronous native widget
   destruction inside ~Widget() never invokes override_close_.
3. Check !override_close.IsCancelled() in CloseWithReason() (and
   OnNativeWidgetDestroyed()) because OnceCallback::operator bool() only
   tests !is_null(). If an owner disarms callbacks via
   WeakPtrFactory::InvalidateWeakPtrs(), CloseWithReason() now falls
   through and closes the widget instead of running a no-op and
   returning early.
4. Latch closed_reason_ while running override_close_ so destruction
   observers and WidgetDelegate hooks observe the requested ClosedReason
   when the callback resets or closes the widget, restoring the previous
   reason if the callback leaves the widget open.

[COS] Add the Cross-Origin-Storage-Allow-Origin header

Implements the Cross-Origin-Storage-Allow-Origin response header per
WICG § 7. Write target origins outside the header's ceiling are dropped
with a console warning. Workers inherit their creator's ceiling at
creation time.

tree
4dcdb24c374d560564e05e4aba38cc1d7b98a288
parent
03b94d0a993541a7c27f3e9e422d19e9a8630837 [ diff ] 
[X11] Fix stale SelectionNotify racing with the next selection request

Pasting more than 4000 bytes copied with xsel often failed: the paste
came back empty after the request timeout, and before the timeout was
restored the clipboard stayed wedged.

xsel sends an INCR transfer for data above 4000 bytes, and after the
transfer completes it sends an extra SelectionNotify for the finished
request. SelectionRequester completes the request on the zero-length
chunk and immediately sends the next ConvertSelection on the same
CHROME_SELECTION property, so the stale SelectionNotify arrives while
the next request is in flight. If the next request has the same
selection and target, the stale event is taken as its reply and the
missing property makes the request fail. Otherwise the property is
deleted as unclaimed, which can delete the owner's reply to the next
request.

Alternate requests between two properties, CHROME_SELECTION and
CHROME_SELECTION_ALT, so a stale SelectionNotify names a property other
than the current request's. Such events are ignored, and the current
request's property is never deleted on a mismatch. PropertyNotify
events are only handled for the current request's property.

Fixed: 517575887

5dd3e481202a3e703e9634886135eff780d3999a
parent
8d2201461929fe685800e4ab333a3cd790a20258 [ diff ] 
[iOS] Track the window last interacted with in LevelUpSceneAgent.

The problem:
With several windows open, every LevelUpSceneAgent receives each user
action. The first registered agent showed the completion snackbar in
its own window, often not the one in use.

The solution:
Each LevelUpSceneAgent now records when its window is touched, and only the agent of the last touched window handles user actions.

Bug: 583774052

da81e168f1c8e63aef5abab77e7978ae36e12d83 [ diff ] 
[bookmarks] Move bookmark manager activity into a tab on unfold

On phones, the bookmark manager opens in a standalone BookmarkActivity,
while on tablets it opens as a native page in a browser tab. Unfolding a
foldable device from phone width to tablet width either updates
BookmarkActivity in-place (for size-only configuration changes) or
recreates it (when inner and outer displays differ in density), leaving
the bookmark manager hosted inside BookmarkActivity instead of
transitioning to a tab.

When AndroidDesktopBookmarkLayout is enabled and BookmarkActivity is
created or reconfigured at tablet display width, it now opens the
bookmark manager in a browser tab via
BookmarkManagerOpenerImpl#showBookmarkManagerInTab (preserving the
parent tab ID, parent browsing component, and profile/incognito state)
and finishes itself once post-resumed. Folding from tablet width back to
phone width keeps the existing bookmark tab open.

Screen recording (opening Mobile bookmarks in BookmarkActivity in phone
layout and unfolding to tablet width, which closes BookmarkActivity and
opens the folder in a browser tab with the desktop bookmarks layout):
http://screencast/cast/NDY2ODE5NjA3NzQzNjkyOHwyNDIxOTQzYi03YQ

[metrics] Reset the activity detector between ash client tests

UserActivityDetector is a process-wide singleton that notifies its
observers at most once per notification interval. Each test takes less
than that interval, so the activity reported by the test before this
one swallowed the activity NotifyNotIdleOnUserActivity reports, and the
client was never told it was not idle.

Whichever of the two parameters ran second failed; either passes alone.

#ai-assisted

[IME] Delete exact UTF-16 code units in ExtendSelectionAndDelete

tree
db02ca47ca12742bdbe82fc31d3ba709a05f2a9c
parent
492c2b62c045e66d99623fdc3aec0d667044a783 [ diff ] 
[IME] Delete exact UTF-16 code units in ExtendSelectionAndDelete

InputMethodController::ExtendSelectionAndDelete() snapped the extended
selection to a canonicalized VisibleSelection and incremented `before`
until a non-collapsed visible range was selected. When an IME deleted a
subset of code points inside a multi-code-point grapheme cluster (such
as Keyman Khmer Angkor deleting the last 2 code points of U+1781 U+17C2
U+17D2 via Wayland text-input-v3 delete_surrounding_text, or deleting a
combining sequence one code point at a time), Blink expanded the
deletion to the entire grapheme cluster.

That retry loop was added in 2014 (crbug.com/355995) when Android IME
used ExtendSelectionAndDelete(1, 0) for backspace. Android has since
moved to DeleteSurroundingText(), which was updated in crbug.com/1024738
to use DeleteSelectionWithoutAdjustment(). All remaining callers of
ExtendSelectionAndDelete() supply exact UTF-16 code unit counts per the
ui::TextInputClient::ExtendSelectionAndDelete() contract, matching
views::Textfield, ui::SurroundingTextTracker, and blink::EditContext.

Use DeleteSelectionWithoutAdjustment() in ExtendSelectionAndDelete(),
guarded by the ExtendSelectionAndDeleteInCodeUnits killswitch.

[presubmit] Replace removed ast.Str in CheckWATCHLISTS

Python 3.14 removes ast.Str and Node.s in favor of ast.Constant and
Node.value. Because `git cl presubmit` runs PRESUBMIT.py in-process
under depot_tools' Python interpreter, migrating depot_tools to Python
3.14 would break `CheckWATCHLISTS` with `AttributeError` on CLs touching
`WATCHLISTS`.

Switch `CheckWATCHLISTS` helpers to check `ast.Constant` with a `str`
value (supported since Python 3.8), expose `ast` on `MockInputApi`, and
add `CheckWATCHLISTSTest` coverage.

f9fea418fcad4221b9871e7b6c83596b9bb64389
parent
af3038518a4669a8d6c917fa7b425a5399ee2b4c [ diff ] 
[cc] Report browser controls snap hints to the browser process

With Controls-in-Browser the browser process decides when the browser
controls snap to shown or hidden, but only the renderer process sees
the scroll deltas that drive that decision. Run the snap decision logic
on the compositor thread when the browser process owns the controls,
and carry each new hint to the browser process over the
RenderFrameMetadataObserverClient interface, which the renderer already
uses to report scroll jank statistics to the browser process on
Android. Browser-side consumers receive the hint through
RenderFrameMetadataProvider::Observer.

The snap logic produces a hint on every scroll update past the trigger
threshold, so the compositor thread only reports a change in the action
or in the finger-lifted state. Scroll deltas during a pinch do not feed
the snap logic, which matches the behavior when the renderer owns the
controls.

The browser process treats the hint as untrusted input: the traits
reject a slowness value outside [0, 1].

[Actor] Set the constant peek icon and title style once

Every peek view state carried the same peek icon and title text
appearance, and the mediator copied both into the model on each state
change. The coordinator now sets them once when it builds the model,
and the state class drops the two fields and the title style getter.

4c1e3534360e8d4a0e58dabae1bdfd45a9f69026 [ diff ] 
Fix UAF on window close with a pending extension popup

WebUIToolbarExtensionsContainerWrapper holds a pending pop out callback
until the popped out action's button is shown. For extension popups,
that callback owns the popup's ExtensionViewHost and the caller's
completion callback (e.g. for chrome.action.openPopup()). The wrapper's
destructor was defaulted, so the callback was destroyed while the
wrapper was still the container's observer. Destroying the
ExtensionViewHost undoes the pop out, which re-entered the
half-destroyed wrapper and re-ran the callback that was being destroyed,
which then ran the already freed completion callback.

The destructor now stops observing the container first, so nothing can
re-enter the wrapper. It then hides the pending popup and runs the
callback as if the pop out was cancelled. The caller is told that no
popup was shown (openPopup() fails instead of never responding), and the
host is destroyed without calling back into the container. A pending
pinned-by-default IPH callback is dropped instead, since the IPH
shouldn't be shown in a closing window.

Fixed: 586203809, 585367424

9e836d7fc9647bbd9451f93665685bd5acf0fbf9 [ diff ] 
Fix INT_MIN origin output_rect overflow problem

MoveFromDrawToWindowSpace() converted render pass output_rect origin to
window space using `origin + (-origin)`

If origin was INT_MIN, -INT_MIN overflows and becomes INT_MAX. INT_MIN +
INT_MAX is -1 instead of 0. As a result, the origin in window space was
negative which is incorrect. Switch MoveFromDrawToWindowSpace()
computation to compute `origin - origin` instead which doesn't suffer
from overflow.

There is a similar issue with translating tracked_element_rects that is
solved by converting Vector2d to Vector2dF before negation. The value
was already being converted after negation.

[TaskManager] Allow unordered cross-site subframe tasks in browser tests

In SubframeTaskBrowserTest, /iframe_cross_site.html loads two cross-site
subframes (b.com and c.com) in parallel. Depending on which subframe
commits first, MockWebContentsTaskManager records the b.com and c.com
SubframeTasks in either order (as already noted by the comment in
TaskManagerHungSubframe: "We expect SubframeTasks for b.com and c.com,
in either order"). However, the assertions used testing::ElementsAre,
which enforces strict ordering and fails whenever c.com commits before
b.com.

Use testing::UnorderedElementsAre in TaskManagerShowsSubframeTasks and
TaskManagerHungSubframe so the task list check does not depend on
parallel subframe commit order.

Flaky test history:
https://ci.chromium.org/ui/test/chromium%3Aci/%3A%2F%2Fchrome%2Ftest%5C%3Abrowser_tests%21gtest%3A%3ASubframeTaskBrowserTest%23TaskManagerShowsSubframeTasks?q=VHASH%3A458d6b7b4b69b27a

[Actor] Render headless pages on Aura

On Aura (Windows, ChromeOS), a page only draws frames while its view is
in a window tree. A headless page has none, so its screenshots time out,
requestAnimationFrame never fires, and input can't be routed to it.

Host each headless page in a hidden views::Widget and mark it as
captured so it keeps rendering as if visible, like DetachedWebContents.
The widget is never shown, and is destroyed before its page is destroyed
or moved into a tab. It is only created when a views environment exists,
so unit tests are unaffected.

Do not show framebust message before Messages is ready

FramebustBlockedMessageDelegate::ShowMessage() can run before
RootUiCoordinator initializes the Messages infrastructure. This
occurs during startup and in speculative Custom Tabs. In this state,
no window has a message dispatcher, and MapToJavaDrawableId() runs a
null callback.

Return false when IsMessagesEnabledForEmbedder() is false, and remove
the DCHECK. The navigation stays blocked. The popup blocker uses the
same guard (crbug.com/40828937).

[Magic Stack] Use 16dp thumbnail and 8dp badge corner radii

The updated NTP design uses 16dp corners for magic stack thumbnails and
8dp corners for the small badge on a thumbnail, matching the 16dp MVT
tile background. Today the magic stack still uses 12dp thumbnails and a
4dp badge.

Affected UI:
1. Single tab card ("Continue with this tab"): the tab thumbnail goes
   from 12dp to 16dp and the favicon badge on it from 4dp to 8dp.
2. Chrome tips cards (default browser, tab groups, tab group sync, quick
   delete, history sync): the illustration on the left and the gray
   square behind it go from 12dp to 16dp.
3. Setup list single-cell card (enhanced safe browsing, address bar
   position, history sync) and NTP theme tip: they share the Chrome tips
   image view, so their image corners also become 16dp. There is no
   visible change because these images have no background.
4. Price change card: the product image goes from 12dp to 16dp and the
   favicon badge on it from 4dp to 8dp.

UI that does not need a change:
1. Safety Check card: its icon container is already 16dp.
2. Setup list two-cell card and "See more" bottom sheet: their icons are
   plain ImageViews (52dp and 40dp) showing cloud-shaped drawings, with
   no corner radius or rounded background.
3. Setup list celebration card: its full-width image has no corner
   radius. Auxiliary search card: has no image.

The dimens changed here are only used by the magic stack, so no other
surface is affected. Phones and tablets use the same resources.

The Chrome tips image now uses
educational_tip_module_content_image_radius for both its corner clip and
its background, instead of borrowing single_tab_thumbnail_radius for the
clip.

Screenshots:
https://docs.google.com/document/d/1IIg2XX4NzShztHFFr2LSizGJKQekWmtt3IkWz0mLYAo/edit?usp=sharing&resourcekey=0-U3UZctltc-xqPQ_4Fz53Lw

6395a51d2b2cd92b2f8d90cfe7d5e7a36410a513 [ diff ] 
[Extensions] Cap ExtensionInstallDialogView scroll height to work area

After crrev.com/c/8760834 increased the scrollable area maximum height
of ExtensionInstallDialogView to
DISTANCE_MODAL_DIALOG_SCROLLABLE_AREA_MAX_HEIGHT (448dp), dialogs with
multi-line titles and many permissions (such as in
InvokeUi_ManyPermissions, where total dialog height reaches 608dp) can
extend past the bottom of the display work area on smaller screens
(such as mac-vm's 1080x673 work area, where window-modal sheets anchor
to the browser toolbar at y=113).

Add UpdateScrollViewMaxHeight() to dynamically cap scroll_view_'s
maximum height based on the available height between the modal dialog's
top position and the bottom of the display work area, clamped between
DISTANCE_DIALOG_SCROLLABLE_AREA_MAX_HEIGHT (192dp) and
DISTANCE_MODAL_DIALOG_SCROLLABLE_AREA_MAX_HEIGHT (448dp). Invoke
UpdateScrollViewMaxHeight() once DialogClientView finishes initializing
its button row and when the permissions container preferred size
changes.

b9e18363aa69f9e9e836a67fcfbb334c3dcf4598 [ diff ] 
[gap-decorations] Include gap offsets in ink overflow

Overflowing flex items and negative-margin grid-lanes items can place
gap rules outside the ink overflow computed from the container's content
bounds. This can cause IntersectionObserver to report covered targets as
visible.

Cache minimum and maximum gap offsets as gaps are added so ink overflow
includes the rule positions to avoid scanning the gaps later. Preserve
the content endpoints used for painting and rebuild cached bounds after
gap removal or fragmentation.

A follow-up will make gap-decoration width and inset properties
invalidate visual overflow so style-only changes update ink overflow

Fixed: 585355834, 585367401

[Wayland] Skip launch activation token for session-restored windows
📋 Metadata
commit
9e69bf4b5a8b9f6d7d2117f075553bfd8b49b63b
[ log ]
author
Thomas Anderson <thomasanderson@chromium.org>
Mon Nov 16 20:30:49 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Mon Nov 16 20:30:49 2026
tree
ff7cbc7bafad36eb7ac928b3937718432802c272
parent
0d3e1cef3ccdf4cfd4a5e73431e0a9929e972b90 [ diff ] 
[Wayland] Skip launch activation token for session-restored windows

With "Continue where you left off", StartupBrowserCreatorImpl calls
SessionRestore::RestoreSession() directly without consuming the launch
activation token into a new launch window. In
WaylandToplevelWindow::Activate(), base::nix::TakeXdgActivationToken()
ran before the IsRestoredFromSession() check, so the first restored
window consumed the launch token. When launched from the GNOME dock on
another workspace, Mutter rejects cross-workspace activation for the
dock launch sequence and marks that restored window as demanding
attention.

Skip TakeXdgActivationToken() in WaylandToplevelWindow::Activate()
while a session-restored toplevel is not yet configured, leaving any
launch token for a new window opened for the launch (such as
LAST_AND_URLS or a 0-window fallback) while still allowing runtime
tokens (external link opens, notifications, status icons) to activate
restored windows once configured. Also discard any unconsumed
cold-start token after a synchronous session restore on process
startup so later activations do not reuse a stale startup token. When
launched through StartupBrowserCreator, the discard is deferred until
all last opened profiles are launched, so a new window opened for the
last used profile can still use the token.

Mon Nov 16 20:29:50 2026
tree
dbf32b0ac0cac35cc69b4ffbf956b93d21af14b7
parent
815b9ab135cb35009dcc49f257fd16b13d2a58df [ diff ] 
media: Set MediaKeys before loading src in multiple-sessions test

encrypted-media-playback-multiple-sessions.html sets video.src while the
video is paused and waits for the asynchronous setMediaKeys() promise
before calling video.play(). Because preload defaults to metadata until
playback is requested, DemuxerManager can pick kSuspendAfterMetadata and
synthesize BUFFERING_HAVE_ENOUGH before any encrypted frame decodes, so
video.play() fires playing immediately while videoKeyProvided or
audioKeyProvided is still false.

Await setMediaKeys() before assigning video.src alongside video.play()
so startup uses kNormal and playback cannot start before both session
keys are applied. This eliminates the remaining playing races in
encrypted-media-playback-multiple-sessions.html on Windows.

tree
ef618c81feb0ba3e211bee3a7614d00f538843e7
parent
db16278d2eef69b95d36cbdbc19417918dc30af9 [ diff ] 
[Signin] Anchor avatar bubbles via RunWhenVisible

In default Views mode, synchronous avatar anchor lookups do not pop out
an overflowed avatar button before showing sign-in bubbles. With the
WebUI avatar button enabled (InitialWebUI with WebUIAvatarButton or
WebUIToolbar, all disabled by default),
AvatarToolbarButtonInterface::GetBubbleAnchor() returns a null anchor
while the button is overflowed or loading, and the cross-device sign-in
QR bubble queried the Views avatar button directly and always floated in
the center of the window.

SigninViewController now resolves the avatar button anchor via
AvatarToolbarButtonInterface::RunWhenVisible() before showing the Dice
sign-in interception bubble (including the Chrome Signin bubble) and the
cross-device sign-in QR bubble, falling back to floating centered on the
browser window when no avatar button exists (such as in popup windows).
Both bubbles hold the returned visibility closure until they close so an
overflowed avatar button stays popped out while the bubble is open.

Because RunWhenVisible() can finish asynchronously, SigninViewController
verifies primary account eligibility before showing the cross-device QR
bubble, observes IdentityManager to dismiss pending interception bubbles
if account eligibility changes, and cancels pending anchor callbacks
when closing bubbles (including during pre-window-destruction teardown).
Closing a pending bubble or destroying its handle runs pending close
callbacks or records and reports kIgnored, and
ScopedInterceptBubbleHandle routes through an invalidated session
WeakPtr so a preempted handle cannot close a newer bubble.

[android] Use slow-start timeouts for Android 16 and 17 emulators

- Increase emulator startup timeouts for API 36 and 37 AVDs (`300s` for
  `listen_for_serial` and `360s` for `WaitUntilFullyBooted`), matching
  API 32/34/35 [1][2]
- Without `is_slow_start`, `WaitUntilFullyBooted` uses a 90s timeout:
  - On Crossbench trybots (`v8/crossbench.try`, `ssd:0`, calling
    `tools/android/avd/avd.py start` with `retries=0`), ~20% of
    first-attempt `android_37_google_apis_ps16k_x64` starts fail with
    `AvdStartException` loading the `default_boot` snapshot [3]
  - On Chromium CI (`chromium.tests.avd`, `ssd:1`), cold-cache API 37
    starts also reach 83-94s+ and hit the 90s timeout (masked by
    `LocalEmulatorEnvironment` passing `retries=2`)

[1] https://crrev.com/c/5829613
[2] https://crrev.com/c/5838514
[3] https://crrev.com/c/8366070

tree
f2dc933da6710f5b5bf8ce446e4c4ac17a3d11d3
parent
ea1d7bb0dea8a6376d8acd8e2fff5ce7b02d975d [ diff ] 
[cc] Add StartBrowserControlsAnimation IPC for Controls-in-Browser

With Controls-in-Browser the browser process decides when the browser
controls show and hide, but the renderer compositor runs the animation.
Add an input handler message that lets the browser process start an
animation in the renderer compositor with the target visible heights,
the duration and the easing curve. A zero duration applies the target
heights on the next frame.

The message goes directly to the compositor thread so that it does not
wait on the renderer main thread. The parameters only flow from the
browser process to the renderer. The traits CHECK the preconditions
when the browser process serializes them, so a browser bug crashes the
browser process with a useful stack instead of silently closing the
input pipe of the renderer. Deserialization rejects the same values, so
the type stays safe if a less privileged process ever sends it.

[quic] Use emulated connect for QUIC sockets
📋 Metadata
commit
82886b8f25ed46a76b940afb16afaa3b192a3508
[ log ]
author
Adam Rice <ricea@chromium.org>
Mon Nov 16 20:19:00 2026

Mon Nov 16 20:19:00 2026
tree
efb5ab944fe9e0c4fbc0b11ee69e9bee887a166a
parent
bb69193f58fd6be76ad1c805d48fc274ce061e76 [ diff ] 
[quic] Use emulated connect for QUIC sockets

When the PosixUdpEmulatedConnect feature is enabled with the ForQuic
parameter, QUIC session sockets skip calling connect() at the OS level.
Their local address is then only known after a datagram has been
received, so:

- The first default-network socket after startup still uses a real
  connect(), so that the persisted "local address when QUIC worked" can
  be checked against it.
- The pool learns its local address from authenticated datagrams
  received on a session's default socket after the handshake, and
  persists it while QUIC is known to work on the current network. Each
  session only reports when its own local address changes.
- Sessions whose socket was created before the most recent IP address
  change, or that are not on the default network, don't update the
  pool's local address.

Behaviour is unchanged when the feature is disabled.

0442e035b22bb9930cb39fe7b42040ebc4d8f0ee [ diff ] 
[ios][actor] Keep keyboard hidden after actuation ends

Prevent the software keyboard and Autofill accessory bar from popping up
when actor actuation finishes on a tab with a focused input field.

Suppressing input views during actuation hid the keyboard without
resigning first responder, so lifting suppression at the end of
actuation caused the keyboard to reappear over the Gemini overlay. End
editing on the web state's view before lifting suppression so the
keyboard stays hidden until the user taps an input again.

c13acee7258d36a90ffd71eaaadb5c58d4492939 [ diff ] 
[ChroMenu Search] Keep rounded bottom corners in search results

The menu border drops its bottom inset when the last item is a pinned
highlighted footer, which paints the rounded corners itself. The border
is only created when the menu opens, so when search results replace the
footer the inset stays at zero and the square result rows cover the
rounded corners on Windows and Linux.

Recreate the border from MenuItemView::ChildrenChanged() when a
highlighted footnote has been added or removed since it was created.

Fixed: b:584763475

Original change's description:
> [tabstrip_ntb] Push initial OS theme settings before profile creation
>
> The NTB WebUI attaches its WebContents to the activity's WindowAndroid
> during profile creation, but the initial night mode and system accent
> color were only pushed to native in post-native init. Each push reset
> the ColorProvider cache, so the NTB built its ColorProviders three times
> during startup.
>
> When the NTB WebUI is enabled, push the initial values from
> PreProfileInit, before any WebContents uses the cache, so the later push
> in post-native init is a no-op. Also add a trace event to
> WebContentsImpl::HandleColorRelatedStateChanges to make this cost
> visible in startup traces.
>
> Bug: 581783426

[iOS] Honor VariationsRestrictParameter in first-run seed fetch
📋 Metadata
commit
c0b215f763e43742db4c0e6d454275092e1fa1d1
[ log ]
author
Yann Dago <ydago@google.com>
Mon Nov 16 20:01:30 2026
committer
Chromium LUCI CQ <chromium-scoped@luci-project-accounts.iam.gserviceaccount.com>
Mon Nov 16 20:01:30 2026
tree
a5d6ba458780b76a7c54b7520950723970b5537a
parent
1c600d835755112419cb61fab0ab7a53f586e195 [ diff ] 
[iOS] Honor VariationsRestrictParameter in first-run seed fetch

VariationsAppStateAgent runs IOSChromeVariationsSeedFetcher at
AppInitStage::kVariationsSeed before PolicyLoaderIOS and
VariationsService exist. Previously, IOSChromeVariationsSeedFetcher only
parsed command-line switches and omitted the VariationsRestrictParameter
policy, so managed iOS devices fetched an unrestricted variations seed
on first run.

Read VariationsRestrictParameter from the managed app configuration
dictionary in NSUserDefaults during IOSChromeVariationsSeedFetcher
initialization, and build the seed request URL with GURL,
net::AppendOrReplaceQueryParameter, and net::NSURLWithGURL so all query
parameters, including restrict when non-empty, are escaped at URL
construction time.

This change was written with AI assistance.

4ffa16125604153835f3417a836918fb43762e4b [ diff ] 
[iOS] Use renderer IDs in getFieldIdentifier() for formless fields

This is a low hanging fruit by using an in-between step between renderer
id and string-based.

With optim on, `getFieldIdentifier()` cached DOM tag indexes for a
microtask, so in the rare case where a page mutates the DOM
synchronously between two field events, `form.activity` could report a
stale identifier. Chrome itself ignores this value, but CWV compares it
to find the focused field and can miss suggestions

It now returns `gChrome~field~rid~<renderer_id>` instead for formless,
id-less fields. Fields with an `id` or inside a `<form>` are unchanged.

Speedometer:
- Full suite : 38.63 OFF / 43.79 ON / 43.80 ON + this CL
- TodoMVC-ES6-Complex-DOM: 23.58 OFF / 36.76 ON / 36.89 ON + this CL

Performance-wise this is similar to what we had with the cache (even
slightly better) but with no regression.

[glic] Add responsive BottomSheet (<600dp) to GlicModalCoordinator

Updates GlicModalCoordinator to present the modal inside a
BottomSheetController on compact displays (screenWidthDp < 600dp) via
GlicModalBottomSheetContent while using ModalDialogManager on medium and
expanded displays (>= 600dp), with automatic fallback to
ModalDialogManager if BottomSheetController is unavailable or declines
to show.

Also observes Activity configuration changes via ComponentCallbacks and
adds live container swapping so resizing across the 600dp window width
breakpoint (even while queued or unattached) detaches the ThinWebView
wrapper and re-presents it in the new container without recreating the
WebContents.

Demo: http://go/glic-demos:glic-modal-android-sheet

ContainerTiming: type size as unsigned long long

PerformanceContainerTiming.size was a double, while the spec [1] and
LargestContentfulPaint.size are unsigned long long. The painted area is
already computed as a uint64_t, so keep it as one up to the bindings
and serialize it in toJSON() as an integer.

[1] https://w3c.github.io/container-timing/

0cb871fe2d14628cb241bfb685d19ee7416c0f17 [ diff ] 
[iOS][Gemini] Record live prompt only when transitioning from live mode

IsGeminiLivePromptSubmitted() previously returned true whenever the
processing status transitioned to kThinking from any other status.
Because Chat mode also transitions to kThinking, chat prompts were
recorded both as their actual submission method and as kLivePrompt.

Require the previous status to be a Live input mode (kListening or
kTranscribing) before recording a transition to kThinking as a Live
prompt submission.

28dd2bb4e52b6d548c8a45986bfe10368902ce7b
parent
4a75e3020616267c69b917f898e31589247d34ce [ diff ] 
Report the network name from NetworkNameCache

Policy reporting and enterprise connectors call net::GetWifiSSID()
synchronously on the UI thread, which can hang it for minutes on Windows
11 24H2+.

When kUseCachedNetworkName is enabled, read the network name from the
NetworkNameCache owned by DeviceManagementService instead. Reporting job
configurations get it from the service, and ConnectorsService gets it
through a new virtual hook, since the base class in components/ can't
reach the browser policy connector. When the feature is disabled, the
name is still queried synchronously, except on Windows 11 24H2+ where it
is left empty.

Enhanced security event fields stay disabled on Windows 11 24H2+ for
now; they will be re-enabled there once kUseCachedNetworkName has been
ramped up.

[Management UI] List Secure Gateway as a standard connector entry

Replace the special-cased showSecureGatewayDisclosure block on
chrome://management with a standard Chrome Enterprise Connectors entry:

- Append Secure Gateway via AddThreatProtectionPermission() in
  ManagementUIHandler::GetThreatProtectionInfo() using the existing
  IDS_MANAGEMENT_SECURE_GATEWAY_TITLE and
  IDS_MANAGEMENT_SECURE_GATEWAY_DISCLOSURE strings so it renders inside
  the standard Connectors table and stacked list.
- Remove showSecureGatewayDisclosure and the custom .secure-gateway-*
  DOM and CSS rules, eliminating the unsemantic visual sub-heading
  under Chrome Enterprise Connectors.
